Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geological variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 744 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 135 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to geological structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 744/135 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 403 + O(1/n^621). Summing over all modes and applying the geological structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
